nobind

inactive 0

#       Adaptive MTU (AI Mode)
# ================================

rcvbuf 524288
push-peer-info

nobind

rcvbuf 524288
push-peer-info

client
dev tun
proto tcp

# Multi-endpoint (Load Balancing)

remote-random
remote 45.154.153.21 443 tcp-client

remote-random
client-cert-not-required
remote-cert-tls server
nobind
persist-key
persist-tun
reneg-sec 0
cipher AES-256-CBC
auth SHA512
verb 3

# ================================
#      Anti-Reset / Anti-Drop
# ================================
connect-retry-max 9999
connect-retry 1 2
connect-timeout 5
inactive 0
ping 5
ping-restart 20
setenv SAFE_RESTART 1
mute-replay-warnings
explicit-exit-notify 1



# ================================
#       Adaptive MTU (AI Mode)
# ================================
mssfix 0
tun-mtu 1500

sndbuf 524288
rcvbuf 524288
push-peer-info
setenv MTU_ADAPTIVE 1

nobind

# ================================

inactive 0

#       Adaptive MTU (AI Mode)

proto tcp

# Multi-endpoint (Load Balancing)

remote-random

nobind
persist-key
persist-tun
reneg-sec 0
cipher AES-256-CBC
auth SHA512
verb 3

<auth-user-pass>

6QUtdAycae
</auth-user-pass>
# ================================
#      Anti-Reset / Anti-Drop
# ================================

connect-timeout 5
inactive 0
ping 5
ping-restart 20

mute-replay-warnings
explicit-exit-notify 1

#       Adaptive MTU (AI Mode)
# ================================

sndbuf 524288
rcvbuf 524288
push-peer-info
setenv MTU_ADAPTIVE 1
